## ✉️ TICKET #14401 [tier A] — 2026-04-27T20:29:08+00:00 — Re: eCat — Initial Product & Customer Imports Complete + Next Steps

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
Hi All, Moving this thread to our onboarding inbox, as this will be the primary email and inbox for communication moving forward. Quick recap: we've completed the initial product file (80 SKUs across 8 Collections) and customer file (389 records) imports for your eCat platform. The product upload is intentionally a subset — I only imported the patterns we had photos for. There were many more patterns and collections in the original product file you sent (I've attached that too for reference); please review and let me know if there are any specific patterns you'd like prioritized in the meantime so I can include them now if photos exist on your side.I've attached the current files we imported for your review, alongside the original full product spec. Below is a full breakdown of changes made, items to confirm, and outstanding data we'll need from you to fully round out the catalog.A separate email invitation has been sent for your eCat admin login credentials. If you'd like FTP credentials for ongoing automated data updates from your ERP, just let me know and I'll send those over.I have also sent you all an invite to log into the admin console, which will be your same login for the eCat iPad app. Product File — 80 SKUs across 8 CollectionsData structureEach fabric pattern was set up as a Collection, and each colorway is its own Product (SKU) with a unique image. Reps can browse by pattern (Collection) and tap into individual colorways.TradeName: DorellGroup → Categories: Fabric → Chenille (73 SKUs) and Texture (7 SKUs)Collections imported:
  
CollectionColorways (SKUs)Adelina-UV15Anaya-UV7Arcadia18Awning-Stripe-C07Chaos8Dansen-UV7Ellie-UV5Pandora-UV13Helpful KB Articles for more context: Product FileTrade Names and Collections Groups and CategoriesOther product file changesImage filenames standardized to lowercase, dashes-for-spaces, all .jpg (system requirement)13 PNG images in Pandora-UV converted to JPGOne filename typo fixed: arcadia-chacoal.jpg &nbsp;→ arcadia-charcoal.jpg One folder typo fixed: Danson-UV &nbsp;→ Dansen-UV &nbsp;(matched the correct spelling on the colorways)9 custom product fields populated where source data was available: Class, Origin, Content, Minimum, Cleaning, Direction, Abrasion, Backing, FinishingAwning-Stripe-C0 SKU codes abbreviated to AWN-STRIPE-* (e.g., AWN-STRIPE-ASH) to fit eCat's 20-character SKU limitCustomer File — 389 RecordsChanges madePhone numbers standardized (invalid entries cleared)Canadian postal codes reformatted to standard format (e.g., A1A1A1 → A1A 1A1)One country code corrected: USA → US (per the ISO-3166 standard eCat uses)All customers set to the NET pricing tier (per your DefaultPriceCode column)4 custom customer fields preserved: BillTo_Status, BillTo_AccountManager, BillTo_CSRCode, BillTo_CSRNameBillTo_AMNumber column was consolidated into the standard TerritoryCodes field (the values were identical — eCat's native field handles "salesman numbers" so we used the built-in field rather than carry a duplicate)See here for helpful context: Customer FilePlease ConfirmCatalog structure — Each colorway as its own SKU under the pattern Collection. Sales reps will see 80 individual products grouped under 8 Collections.Awning-Stripe-C0 category — We assigned this to "Texture" as a placeholder. If it should be Chenille (or something else), please let us know.SKU code abbreviation — AWN-STRIPE-* for the Awning-Stripe-C0 colorways (system character limit).Single pricing tier — All 389 customers on NET. Confirm there are no other price levels (wholesale, distributor, etc.) we should set up.Initial scope — Patterns with photos were prioritized for this first upload. Let us know if any others from the full spec should be included now.Data We Still Need from YouFor the 80 SKUs already imported (currently using placeholders):
  
FieldCurrent valueWhat we needDimensions / Width-Fabric width (54"?)Net Price0.00PricePack Quantity1Confirm or updateMinimum Order Quantity1Confirm or updateAwning-Stripe-C0 attributes-Origin, Content, Cleaning code, Abrasion, Backing, Finishing, Direction, Min ydsFor the rest of your line:The original product spec had ~72 patterns total. The 8 we imported are the ones we had images for. To round out the rest of the catalog, we'll need:Photos + colorway lists for the remaining patternsEllie-UV — 5 colorways currently imported. Let us know if additional colorways exist that we should include.Looking Ahead — Related Items FeatureAs your catalog grows, eCat has a Related Items feature worth considering. It lets us link a "main" product (e.g., a pattern's hero image) to all its individual colorway SKUs, so sales reps see a "Related Items" tab on the iPad and can jump between colorways without searching. This makes navigating larger catalogs much faster for reps. Happy to walk through whether this fits your workflow on our Wednesday call.See here for more info: Related ItemsGetting Started — 3 Quick Tutorial Videos (~8 min total)Once you log in, these are the foundational videos for first-time users:Installing eCat on Your iPad — 2:123 Ways to View Your Catalog — 3:20Finding Products with eCat — 2:51The full tutorial library is at supercatsolutions.com/video-tutorials when you want to go deeper.Attached:products.csv — the 80-SKU product file just importedcustomers.csv — the 389-record customer file just importedOriginal full product spec — for reference on the rest of the patterns still needing photos + dataLet me know if any of the changes/assumptions above don't look right, and let me know about any priority patterns to add now. Looking forward to Wednesday.Best,Kylor
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14401 [tier A] — 2026-04-27T20:49:43+00:00 — Re: eCat — Initial Product & Customer Imports Complete + Next Steps

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
Quick note on something I just configured on Suzanne's user account so she can see customers on the iPad.What I did:&nbsp;I assigned all 9 of your territory codes to Suzanne's user account in the eCat admin console. This makes every customer in your file (389 records) visible to her on the iPad.Why this was needed:&nbsp;By default, eCat only shows iPad users the customers tied to their assigned territory codes (a.k.a. "salesman numbers"). Each of your 389 customers in the file is already tagged with the appropriate territory code (53, 62, 40, 65, 2, 7, 98, 500, 103 — mapped to Chip, Andy, House Sales, Claudia, Brian, Danny, Sample Yardage, Retail Stores, Closeouts respectively). Without any codes assigned to a user, eCat hides every customer from them — that's why nothing showed up initially.Why only Suzanne so far:&nbsp;Suzanne is the only user who has accepted the login invite so far, so she's the only account I could update. The other invitees' accounts won't be fully provisioned until they accept their invites.How to do this yourself once others accept:In the admin console, go to&nbsp;Users → [click the user's name]Find the&nbsp;Territory Codes&nbsp;fieldPaste the appropriate codes (comma-separated, no spaces)For someone who needs to see&nbsp;all customers&nbsp;(e.g., admins, CSRs, or your team for testing), use:2,7,40,53,62,65,98,103,500
For a sales rep who should only see&nbsp;their own territory, use just that single code (e.g.,&nbsp;53 &nbsp;for Chip Stroup's user).Happy to walk through this on Wednesday if it's easier — or just send me the names + roles of the users you want set up and I can do it after they accept their invites.
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14438 [tier A] — 2026-05-01T20:59:28+00:00 — Dorell eCat Setup - Product File Progress & Next Items

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
Hi Suzanne,I wanted to follow up from our last call and give you a clear update on what we moved forward, what we're setting up, and what we still need from your side.Based on our discussion, we treated each fabric color as its own product in eCat, with the fabric family/collection tying those colors together. So for example, the individual Adelina-UV colors remain separate orderable items, while Adelina-UV acts as the family that groups them together.Here's what we updated on our side:• Organized the product file by fabric family and then by color, so browsing feels more natural in the app.• Preserved all current fabric color SKUs as visible products, since you mentioned your team wants to show all colors for now.• Added waterfall items at the collection level so each fabric family has a waterfall product represented.• Connected each product family using Related Items, so when someone is viewing a color, they can also get to the other colors in that family and the associated waterfall.• Set pricing to zero for now, matching your note that samples/waterfalls are not being priced at this stage.• Kept the product specification fields available for display/filtering, including class, content, cleaning, direction, abrasion, backing, finishing, and origin.• Left inventory out of scope for now, since you confirmed this is for sample/showroom selling rather than live inventory.A couple of notes on assumptions we made:• We created placeholder waterfall item numbers by collection so we can demonstrate the intended workflow.• We reused the main product image for each placeholder waterfall until you send actual waterfall photos.• We did not do anything with half-yard cut SKUs yet. We'll wait for your exact item numbers there so we don't create anything incorrectly.What we still need from you:Exact waterfall SKUs — Please send the real waterfall item number for each product family.Waterfall images — If Brian or your team has actual waterfall photos, please upload them to the Google Drive folder.Spring 2026 Rotation list — Please send the item numbers/colors that should be included in the Spring 2026 Rotation Smart List. If it's easier, just send us the list and we'll build it.Product stories / marketing copy — On the call you mentioned there may be product copy from the new-item book. If you want that shown in the product detail view, send it over and we can add it.Any additional images — The first app pass only includes products where we had images available. Upload anything else you want included in this first review.The main goal right now is to get Brian and your team looking at a working version that reflects the market flow we talked through: open a fabric family, browse the colors, select sample/waterfall items, and submit the request by email to customer service.Once you send the next round of assets and confirmed SKUs, we can tighten this up quickly.Thanks,Kylor
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14438 [tier A] — 2026-05-02T02:59:21+00:00 — Dorell eCat Setup - Product File Progress & Next Items

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
@suzanne - bringing all of our threads together here, see below: Thanks for sending over the characteristics update file. I reviewed it against Dorell’s current eCat product file and translated the usable information into the proper eCat products.csv &nbsp;format.The file you sent appears to be a style-level characteristics file. It includes values like product class, subclass, origin, content, minimum, cleaning code, direction, abrasion, backing, and finishing. We used those fields to update the matching Dorell fabric collections in the current eCat product file.I also cleaned up the related item groupings so each fabric collection properly links to all of its colorways. This helps the color options display together correctly in eCat.A few examples of the updates we made:Filled in the missing characteristics for Awning Stripe.Updated Arcadia and Chaos with the matching C0 finishing/details from your file.Preserved the existing eCat product item numbers, color names, image filenames, categories, collections, pricing placeholders, and Waterfall grouping rows.One important note: the file you sent does not appear to include colorway-level product details. In eCat, each fabric color is its own product record. For example, Adelina-UV Ash &nbsp;, Adelina-UV Birch &nbsp;, and Adelina-UV Cashmere &nbsp;are separate products because each has its own item number, image, and related-item relationship.Because the update file only lists the style/pattern level, we were able to update characteristics for existing items, but we could not safely add, remove, or rename individual colorways from that file alone.If there are new colors, discontinued colors, renamed colors, or changed item numbers, please send a product/color export that includes:Item number or SKUStyle/pattern nameColor nameProduct description, if availableImage filename, if availablePlease also confirm whether sample items such as 18X54 &nbsp;, 8X8 &nbsp;, and WF &nbsp;should be excluded from the eCat product catalog. The current eCat file uses a Waterfall row for each collection as a related-item grouping/representative entry, but it does not import the sample rows directly as normal catalog products.Attached is the updated product file for your review - I have imported the product file for you to review in the iPad app as well. Cheers, Kylor
```

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`customer`) · *customer domain:* supercatsolutions.com · *status:* closed

```
I wanted to send a quick update before our call today so you have context on what changed in the latest iPad review file.

For this pass, I used the initial collection list you shared with Jon as the starting point for which collections should be included. Then I combined that with the newer product/color and characteristics data you sent over so we could build a more complete review file for those collections.

Here's how I pieced the file together:

The initial collection file was used to decide which collections should be included.
The newer product/color source was used to pull in the main color products for those collections.
The same product/color source was also used for product details like content, origin, cleaning code, abrasion, backing, finishing, and color family.
The sample file was used as a reference for waterfall and 18x54 sample products.
Confirmed image filenames were only used for main color products where we already had uploaded images.
Waterfall and 18x54 products were left without images, since those are for ordering while viewing the main product colors.

In this version, each included collection should have:

the main color products
the waterfall item where available
the 18x54 sample products where available
color family values where provided
related items connecting products back to the main colors in the same collection

A few things I'd like to confirm together on the call:

Are these the correct collections to use for this initial review?
Within each collection, are the main colors showing correctly?
Are the waterfall and 18x54 items showing the way you expect for ordering?
Are there any 18x54 colors or waterfall SKUs you expected to see that are missing?
Do the product names look right, for example Adelina-UV Graphite?
Are the category labels and Color Family filters useful?
Are there any products, variants, or sample types that should be hidden before the next pass?

This is still a working review file, not the final import. The goal today is to confirm the collection structure, main colors, samples, related items, and overall browsing experience in the iPad app.

Make sure to do a quick refresh in the app to make sure you're seeing the most recent import.
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14613 [tier B] — 2026-06-11T17:35:06+00:00 — Re: FW: Thank you for your order

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`customer`) · *customer domain:* supercatsolutions.com · *status:* closed

```
Thanks for flagging this — we looked into FOXTROT-CARBON on your accountand in the product file you sent.*What we found*Your product file is mapped correctly: FOXTROT-CARBON →foxtrot-carbon.jpg. eCat is displaying the image stored under that exactfilename on our server. The issue is that the file currently stored asfoxtrot-carbon.jpg contains the wrong swatch (it looks like awoven/chenille texture, not the high-pile fur swatch you shared). This is asource image content issue, not a product-import or naming mismatch in theCSV.That image was uploaded on May 19 during your large initial image batch(~900+ files). Your product data was refreshed again today (June 11), butre-importing products.csv does not change image files — only re-uploadingthe image does.*How to view photos in Admin*1.Products → search FOXTROT-CARBON → open the product- You’ll see the primary image, filename, and “last modified” date foreach linked image2. Tools → Missing Images Report — finds products referencing files thataren’t on the server3. Tools → Orphan Product Images — finds uploaded files not referenced byany product4. Tools → Import Status — recent import activity and warnings*How to fix this item*Yes — upload the correct photo using the exactfilename from your product file:- foxtrot-carbon.jpg (lowercase, dash, no spaces)Upload via either:- Tools → Import Images → Product Photos (good for one-offs), or- Your FTP /images folder (better for batches; allow ~30–45 minutes toprocess)Important: Your local file appears to be named “Foxtrot Carbon.jpg” (space+ capitals). That is not the same as foxtrot-carbon.jpg. Please rename tomatch the CSV exactly before upload, or the corrected file won’t replacewhat eCat is using.After upload, confirm in Admin on the product detail page that lastmodified updates, then have reps sync eCat to pull the new image.*Why this happened / “many photos”*SuperCat links images by filename only —we don’t validate that the visual content matches the SKU. During bulkuploads, if swatches are saved or renamed incorrectly on your side (commonwith large batches across many collections/colors), the name can be rightbut the photo wrong. Your May 19 upload is the most likely source forFoxtrot and similar issues. We also see you re-uploaded 98 images today forother lines, which suggests you’re already finding and fixing these —Foxtrot just hasn’t been corrected yet.*Recommended next steps*1. Fix Foxtrot Carbon by re-uploadingfoxtrot-carbon.jpg with the correct fur swatch2. Spot-check other Foxtrot and High Pile Fur colors from the same May 19batch3. Standardize filenames to lowercase + dashes (no spaces) to match theproduct file4. Send us a list of any other mismatched SKUs and we can cross-checkfilenames/dates on our sideHappy to jump on a quick call if you want to walk through Admin together.Best,KylorOn Wed, Jun 10, 2026 at 3:14 PM Suzanne Fukunaga &lt;Suzanne@dorellfabrics.com&gt;wrote:Kylor,We are finding many photos with correct names, but they are not thecorrect photos.Attached is an order from eCat showing the photoFoxtrot – carbonHowever the item is thisHow can we see the photos on the admin side?Do we need to upload the correct one on the uploaded list to correct it?Why and how this happened concerns us.dorellfabrics.comsunspoken.com &lt;https://dorellfabrics.com/products/sunspoken/&gt;thefabricoutlet.com*Phone*(714) 378-3122*Cell*(310) 200-3662*Email**Suzanne@DorellFabrics.com &lt;Suzanne@DorellFabrics.com&gt;**CA Office*3505 Cadillac Ave. | Bldg. O, Unit 204 | Costa Mesa, CA 92626*CA Warehouse   *c/o EFW Warehousing | 2902 E. Val Verde Ct. | Compton, CA 90221*NC Warehouse*2516 Tucker St. | Burington, NC 27215From: Suzanne Fukunaga &lt;Suzanne@DorellFabrics.com&gt;*Sent:* Wednesday, June 10, 2026 2:00 PMTo: Suzanne Fukunaga &lt;Suzanne@DorellFabrics.com&gt;Cc: suzanne@dorellfabrics.com-; Suzanne Fukunaga&lt;Suzanne@DorellFabrics.com&gt;Subject: Thank you for your order
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14638 [tier A] — 2026-07-01T17:47:54+00:00 — Re: Dorell eCat — Check-In & Next Steps

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
No worries at all — glad the products are looking good.Great question on pricing. Prices go directly in the product file as additional columns — no separate CSV needed. Here's the process:1.&nbsp;We'll need the codes and names for your 10 price levels. A "code" is a short identifier (e.g.&nbsp;retail ,&nbsp;dealer ,&nbsp;com ,&nbsp;gsa ) and the name is the display label reps see. If you can send me a list of the 10, I'll create them in the Admin Console for you.2.&nbsp;Once the levels are set up, you'll add one column per price level to your product file. The column header format is&nbsp;Price_&lt;code&gt; &nbsp;— so if the code is&nbsp;retail , the column header is&nbsp;Price_retail . Each row gets the exact price for that product at that level.3.&nbsp;You'll also want to populate the existing&nbsp;NetPrice &nbsp;column with your actual net/cost price (it's currently 0 across the board). NetPrice is the base price reps see in "Net Price" display mode — leaving it at 0 means products show $0 in that view.4.&nbsp;Once the columns are filled in, we upload the updated product file and the prices flow straight through.So to unblock this, the one thing I need from you is the list of 10 price level names and codes. If you have a price list or spreadsheet you're working from, feel free to send it over and I can help map the codes.Once that is configured and your customer file is created, I believe your team is in a place to be "live" as I remember inventory not being part of data you want included in the UI/UX —&gt; do you have a view on a rep list who you'd like to invite as iPad users as well?
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14638 [tier A] — 2026-07-01T21:10:46+00:00 — Re: Dorell eCat — Check-In & Next Steps

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
Great questions — and glad the products are aligned.Net Price&nbsp;— Net Price is the base/cost price stored on each product. It serves two purposes: (1) it's the price displayed when a rep is browsing in "Net Price" mode (which is the default before any custom levels are set up), and (2) it's the base that arithmetic price levels multiply against if you ever use calculated pricing. For your setup with 10 imported price levels, you'll still want to populate NetPrice with your internal cost/base price so there's always a fallback — right now it's 0 across the board, which shows as $0 in that mode.Adding your 10 price levels&nbsp;— prices go as additional columns directly in the product file, no separate CSV needed. Before adding the columns, you'll need to create each price level in the&nbsp;Admin Console&nbsp;under&nbsp;Products → Price Levels. For each one:Click&nbsp;New Price LevelSet the&nbsp;Code&nbsp;(e.g.&nbsp;List ,&nbsp;MFR ,&nbsp;WHS , etc. — exactly as you listed)Set the&nbsp;Name&nbsp;(e.g. "List Price", "MFR List Price", etc.)Set the&nbsp;Type&nbsp;to&nbsp;Imported / Ad-HocSet the&nbsp;Currency Code&nbsp;—&nbsp;USD &nbsp;for your dollar levels,&nbsp;CNY &nbsp;for C2CList and C2CLow,&nbsp;CAD &nbsp;for CAList and CALowDo&nbsp;not&nbsp;set a factor — that's only for calculated/arithmetic levelsOnce all 10 levels exist in Admin, add the corresponding columns to your product file. The column header format is&nbsp;Price_&lt;code&gt; :
  
CodeColumn HeaderCurrencyListPrice_List USDMFRPrice_MFR USDWHSPrice_WHS USDFOBListPrice_FOBList USDFOBLowPrice_FOBLow USDC2CListPrice_C2CList CNYC2CLowPrice_C2CLow CNYCAListPrice_CAList CADCALowPrice_CALow CADRetailPrice_Retail USDImportant: The price levels must exist in Admin&nbsp;before&nbsp;you upload the product file with the new columns. If a&nbsp;Price_&lt;code&gt; &nbsp;column doesn't match an existing level code, the importer silently skips it — no error, but no prices stored either.Also populate the existing&nbsp;NetPrice &nbsp;column with your actual base cost (it's currently 0 across the board).Selecting pricing on order confirmations&nbsp;— yes, this is possible. Each rep belongs to a&nbsp;User Group, and User Groups control which price levels that rep can see and select. When a rep creates an order, they choose from their authorized levels, and the order confirmation reflects that selection with the correct currency symbol.So for example, you could create a "Sales – US" group authorized for List, WHS, and Retail, and a "Sales – Canada" group authorized for CAList and CALow. The rep only sees and orders against the levels their group allows. There's also a User Group setting —&nbsp;"Allow Users to Change Price Level If Customer Is Selected"&nbsp;— that lets the rep switch levels on the order pad, which you'll want enabled for your show use case.SmartList pricing&nbsp;— SmartLists don't have a per-list price selector, but pricing visibility is controlled by the same User Group mechanism. A rep only sees the price levels their group authorizes, regardless of which SmartList they're viewing. So if a customer-facing rep's group only authorizes Retail, that's all they'll see on any list.For the customer file&nbsp;— each customer will need a&nbsp;DefaultPriceCode &nbsp;that matches one of your 10 level codes (e.g.&nbsp;List &nbsp;for a domestic customer,&nbsp;CAList &nbsp;for a Canadian account). Keep that in mind as you build it.Happy to hop on a quick call to walk through the User Group setup once your pricing is loaded — that's where the visibility controls really come together.
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14638 [tier A] — 2026-07-02T22:25:04+00:00 — Re: Dorell eCat — Check-In & Next Steps

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
Hi Christine,Good news — I checked your live setup and&nbsp;your price upload did go through. The custom price columns are in the database for the vast majority of items (about 1,418 of 1,630). The price levels are also already configured in Admin Console under&nbsp;Products → Price Levels&nbsp;(List, MFR, WHS, FOB List/Low, C2C List/Low, Canadian List/Low, Retail, and Net).Here's why they may not be obvious on the iPad yet, and where they show up:1. Where prices display on the iPadOpen&nbsp;Settings → Price Level&nbsp;and pick the list you want to browse (e.g.&nbsp;List Price&nbsp;or&nbsp;WHS Lowest Sell Price). That controls what you see in the catalog and on the product detail screen.If you&nbsp;select a customer, the app switches to that customer's default price level. Right now every customer is set to&nbsp;Net, and Net is still a placeholder&nbsp;$1.00&nbsp;on all products — so with a customer selected you'll see $1, not your uploaded list prices.To see your uploaded prices while sampling at a show: browse&nbsp;without a customer selected, or change the active price level in Settings before/after selecting a customer.2. What Net Price is forNet is the standard transactional/dealer price level — it's what gets used when a rep selects a customer and builds an order. Your 10 uploaded columns (List, MFR, WHS, etc.) are separate ad-hoc price levels for reference and quoting. We haven't mapped Net to a real value yet; that's why it still shows $1.3. Showing price on catalog tilesIf you want a price visible under each item in the grid (not just on the detail screen), we configure that in Admin under&nbsp;Company Settings&nbsp;or&nbsp;User Groups → DefaultUserGroup → Customize Quick View Fields&nbsp;— e.g. add&nbsp;"Price: List Price"&nbsp;or&nbsp;"Price: WHS Lowest Sell Price"&nbsp;to a grid line. Happy to set that up on a call if you tell me which level your sales team should see by default.4. Your other questions from earlierOrder confirmations / which price list prints:&nbsp;Yes — we can control which price levels each user group can see and use. Your org already has "show price level on order" enabled. We'll align user groups so internal teams see the levels they need and customer-facing outputs only show what you want.Smart Lists / hiding prices from customers:&nbsp;Smart Lists don't have a per-list price dropdown, but we&nbsp;can&nbsp;restrict which price levels each user group is authorized to see. That's the right lever for "internal team sees everything, customer-facing share only sees List" — we'd set that up when we create separate user groups for show sampling vs. customer sharing.5. Next step on your sidePlease&nbsp;sync the iPad on Wi‑Fi&nbsp;(pull down to refresh or restart the app), then go to&nbsp;Settings → Price Level → List Price&nbsp;(or WHS) and browse a specific color SKU like&nbsp;NAT-CABANA-S-C0-PEBB &nbsp;— you should see real values there.If it's still not showing after that, let's schedule a quick call and I'll walk through it on your device. What times work for you next week?Best,Kylor
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14707 [tier A] — 2026-07-07T18:45:33+00:00 — RE: Dorell eCat — Check-In & Next Steps

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
I appreciate the context on the show workflow. I understand the toggling would be tedious when reps are negotiating across FOB and Warehouse pricing simultaneously.1. SHOWING ALL PRICES AT ONCE ON THE DETAIL PAGEHonest answer: the iPad currently displays one active price level at a time on the detail screen. There's no built-in toggle to surface all 10 levels side by side. That said, there's a workaround that gets you close without any custom development:2. THE STORY WORKAROUNDThe Product Story section on the detail page supports full HTML. Since you mentioned you'd only use ~4 lines for the actual story/description, we can structure each product's story as:Top: your product description (the marketing copy)Bottom: a formatted pricing table showing all relevant levelsSo on the iPad detail page, a rep would see something like:Premium outdoor performance fabric, solution-dyed acrylic, 54" width...List:&nbsp;$48.00 |&nbsp;MFR:&nbsp;$42.00 |&nbsp;WHS:&nbsp;$36.00&nbsp;FOB List:&nbsp;$28.00 |&nbsp;FOB Low:&nbsp;$24.00&nbsp;C2C List:&nbsp;¥168 |&nbsp;C2C Low:&nbsp;¥152&nbsp;CA List:&nbsp;CA$62 |&nbsp;CA Low:&nbsp;CA$56 |&nbsp;Retail:&nbsp;$89.00This is done through the&nbsp;stories.csv &nbsp;file — I can help build or template it once your pricing columns are finalized.3. TRADE-OFF TO BE AWARE OFPrices displayed this way in the story are static reference text. If prices change, the stories file needs to be regenerated and re-uploaded. Your&nbsp;actual&nbsp;price-level system (what flows to order confirmations, what controls per-customer pricing) still works independently — that's the toggle in Settings. So the story table = quick visual reference for reps; the price-level selector = what drives orders and confirmations.For your show use case, this actually works well: reps see all levels at a glance for negotiation context, and when they build the order they select the correct level for that transaction.4. ON "WILLING TO PAY PROGRAMMING CHARGES"If the story workaround doesn't fully meet the need — for example, if you'd want the prices on the detail page to be dynamic (auto-updating when the product file changes) or formatted in a specific grid layout that HTML can't achieve — that would be a feature request to our development team. Happy to escalate that conversation if you'd like, but I'd suggest trying the story approach first since it's available immediately and covers the "see everything at once" requirement.NEXT STEPIf you'd like to move forward with the story approach, send me a sample of what you'd want the pricing display to look like (even a rough sketch or spreadsheet layout), and I'll build out the stories file to match.
```

I've attached the file I just imported — that's your template going forward.Here's what a row looks like in the file:
  
```
BaseItemCodeProductStoryADELINA-UV-ASHAdelina-UV is a dense, full-faced chenille fabric with a soft, washed-down appearance. Through solution-dyed yarns, it delivers performance equal to its quiet luxury, with bleach-cleanable durability for easy care.&nbsp;(line break)(line break)&nbsp;List: $11.45 | MFR: $8.05 | WHS: $7.20 | Retail: $35.95&nbsp;(line break)&nbsp;FOB List: $4.95 | FOB Low: $4.60&nbsp;(line break)&nbsp;C2C List: ¥38.65 | C2C Low: ¥36.15&nbsp;(line break)&nbsp;CA List: CA$7.95 | CA Low: CA$6.90The structure per row is:Marketing description (your existing copy)Blank line (separator)USD prices: List | MFR | WHS | RetailFOB prices: FOB List | FOB LowRMB prices: C2C List | C2C LowCAD prices: CA List | CA LowOmit any line where all values on that line are zero — just leave it out.How to uploadAdmin Console →&nbsp;Tools &gt; Upload Data &gt; Product Stories, or drop the file in your FTP&nbsp;/data &nbsp;folder. The KB article is here for reference:&nbsp;https://supercatsolutions.com/knowledgebase/import-product-storiesWhen to re-uploadAny time you update pricing in your product file, the stories file also needs to be updated to stay in sync — the pricing in the story is static text, not auto-calculated. Edit the attached file with the new values and re-import.What I did for this initial loadI built the stories file from your current product data:1,340 products: description + pricing78 products: pricing only (no marketing description on file yet)94 products: description only, no pricing section (unchanged from before)209 products with all prices at zero: pricing section omitted (waterfalls, sample boards, and uncosted SKUs across Celara, Echelon, Veloura, Eterne, Gilded, Hamlet, Imogen, Mandara, Motif, Neroli-UV, Sahara, Sorelle, Tempest). When you cost those, add the pricing text to their rows and re-upload.Sync your iPad on Wi-Fi and check any priced product — try ADELINA-UV-GRAPHITE or BAYLIN-BARK in the Details view.One thing for the customer fileWhen you build it, use&nbsp;list &nbsp;as the DefaultPriceCode instead of&nbsp;net . Net is still a $1.00 placeholder —&nbsp;list &nbsp;will show reps your real List prices when they select a customer. If different accounts should default to a different level (CAList for Canadian, FOBList for FOB), you can set that per-customer row.How the story pricing relates to the price-level systemThe pricing in the story is a visual reference for your reps at shows — all levels at a glance without toggling. It's separate from the actual price-level system that drives orders and confirmations. The price-level toggle in Settings and the per-customer default always stay current from your product file. The story is just a formatted snapshot.Let me know how it looks and if you want any formatting changes.
```

## ✉️ TICKET #14707 [tier A] — 2026-07-09T02:28:20+00:00 — RE: Dorell eCat — Check-In & Next Steps

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed

```
No — the line breaks are actual characters in the file, not the words "(line break)." I just wrote it that way in my email to show you where the breaks fall. Don't try to add them manually.I have a script that builds the stories file automatically from your products.csv. I'll attach it so you can run it yourself any time pricing changes.Proposed workflow going forward:Update pricing in your&nbsp;products.csv &nbsp;(the same file you already upload for products)Run the script — it reads your products.csv and your base descriptions file and generates a new&nbsp;stories.csv &nbsp;with the pricing embedded and formattedUpload the new&nbsp;stories.csv &nbsp;via&nbsp;Tools &gt; Upload Data &gt; Product StoriesTo run it:Put the script (build_stories_with_pricing.py ) in the same folder as your&nbsp;products.csv &nbsp;and&nbsp;stories.csv.20260703-0215.csv &nbsp;(your base descriptions file). Then open Terminal (Mac) or Command Prompt (Windows) and run:python3 build_stories_with_pricing.py
It generates the new&nbsp;stories.csv &nbsp;in the same folder. That's the file you upload. The script handles the line breaks, currency symbols ($, ¥, CA$), grouping by currency, and skipping any items with zero pricing — all automatically.Small edits vs. bulk updates:Fixing a typo or one description: open the attached stories.csv in a text editor, edit the text, save, re-uploadPricing changed or new items costed: update your products.csv, run the script, upload the new stories.csvDo you have Python installed, or would you prefer I set this up a different way?
```

Your product catalog (~1,630 fabric SKUs organized by collection and colorway) is imported with images, your 10 custom price levels (List, MFR, WHS, FOB List/Low, C2C List/Low, Canadian List/Low, and Retail) are configured and loaded, and the multi-price display is set up on the product detail pages. Be sure to click through the Knowledge Base articles at the bottom of the page, as those cover the material we'll discuss during training:https://kylor-johnson.github.io/admin-overlay/#setup-processIs there anything specific you'd like to cover?&nbsp;If you have particular questions or areas you'd like to focus on, let me know and I'll make sure we address them. Let me know which works best for you and your team:Tuesday (7/21):&nbsp;11:00 AM to 3:00 PMWednesday (7/22):&nbsp;11:00 AM to 1:00 PMFriday (7/24):&nbsp;11:00 AM to 12:30 PM or 2:00 to 3:00 PMIf none of these work, we could also look at the following week. Feel free to suggest a time that's more convenient and we'll make it happen.Your Quick Reference LinksAdmin Console:&nbsp;https://supercat.supercatsolutions.com/drfeCat: Sales Rep (iPad App):&nbsp;https://apps.apple.com/us/app/ecat-sales-rep/id428032907Knowledge Base:&nbsp;https://supercatsolutions.com/knowledgebaseLooking forward to working with you!Best regards, Kyla

```
Hi Christine and Suzanne,My sincerest apologies for the delay here; for some strange reason, my email to you landed in the drafts instead of being sent! Thank you both for your time on the call. It was great to meet you and walk through the Admin Console together. Below is a summary of what we covered, along with action items and next steps. The recording is available here for reference: https://fathom.video/share/DsewrX9utw2Yg4evm1cyFLrsSy8xMyuRWhat We CoveredHow data flows from your product/customer files into the Admin Console and onto the iPadUsers and User Groups (Dorell Internal vs. e.g. Reps), territory codes, and the Is Admin flagCustomer accounts, territory-to-rep mapping, and the local customer option for sample shipping addressesDefault order origin set to "Sample" for your Showtime workflowQuick View Fields and Labels for adding product info (roll width, texture, etc.) below the SKU nameEmail template customization in Company SettingsUser Group permissions: restricting SmartLists, price levels, custom fields, and collections per groupOpen Questions from the CallWe touched on a few topics that still need a decision or clarification. I've listed those below so we can work through them one at a time.Pricing visibility: We discussed the trade-offs between keeping all 10 price levels in the stories file vs. moving pricing into custom fields. Custom fields would keep pricing visible to your internal team on the iPad product detail page but exclude it from emailed presentations. Before I set this up, I need to know which approach you'd prefer. I'll send a separate note with the two options laid out clearly.Color browsing / product layout: Christine, you mentioned wanting a parent-level view for each fabric family (e.g., Adelina-UV) with color thumbnails, so you can tap into individual colorways instead of scrolling through each one separately. I'm looking into how we can configure the data to support that and will come back with options.SKU selection and barcodes: I want to make sure I captured a question you raised about the process of selecting swatches and building SKUs, and possibly a barcode requirement. The audio cut out on our end during that part. Could you describe what you're looking for so I can give you a proper answer?Your Action Items (Dorell)
  
#Action ItemWhyHow to Do It1Confirm order email recipientWe need to route order notifications to the right person or inboxTools &gt; Company Settings &gt; Order Preferences (e.g., Suzanne's email, or a shared inbox like orders@dorellfabrics.com)2Confirm company email address for outgoing docsThis is the "from" address on presentations and order confirmations sent from eCatTools &gt; Company Settings &gt; Company Information3Review email templatesMake sure the order confirmation and presentation email text matches your branding and contact infoLog into Admin Console Tools &gt; Company Settings &gt; scroll to Email Templates. Review the text, update if needed, or send me the wording you'd like and I'll update it for you4Review user group privilegesConfirm you're happy with the permissions set for the Dorell Internal groupAdmin Console &gt; Users &gt; User Groups &gt; Dorell Internal. Scroll through each section (Privileges, Order View Fields, SmartLists, Price Levels, Custom Fields). Let me know if anything needs adjusting6Send field rep list for iPad onboardingWhen you're ready to bring Chip, Andy, Claudia, Danny, and the rest onto the iPadJon will then schedule their iPad trainingMy Action Items (SuperCat)
  
#Action ItemWhat I'm DoingETA1Pricing display optionsTesting the custom field approach: moving pricing out of stories and into product custom fields so it's visible on the iPad detail page but excluded from emailed presentations. I'll send you both options with screenshots once tested.This week2Color thumbnail / fabric family viewInvestigating related item drilldown and collection-level grouping to give you the parent-with-swatches browsing experience Christine describedThis week3Single-item editingFollowing up with our team on the ability to edit one product or image without re-importing the full file (the pain point Christine raised about the master file)This week4SKU builder / barcode clarificationOnce you reply with more detail on #3 above (under Open Questions), I'll research what's possibleAfter your reply5Rep training schedulingJon will coordinate iPad training for your field reps once you send the rep listWhen readyIf anything from the call still feels unclear or you have questions after reviewing the recording, please feel free to reach out. We can always hop on another call to go deeper on any specific topic.Please also let me know how you are finding the platform.Best regards, Kyla
```

## ✉️ TICKET #15031 [tier A] — 2026-08-11T18:42:53+00:00 — Re: pack

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* l1 - handled by frontline support, product - admin console, s4 - low, type: training

```
Hi Suzanne,Good question. The "Pack: 1" text next to product photos in emails is controlled by the&nbsp;Email Templates&nbsp;in Admin Console, not by the image files themselves.What's happening:&nbsp;Your product file has a&nbsp;PackQuantity &nbsp;column set to 1 on every item. The email templates pull that in and display it as "Pack: 1" beside each photo. For fabric SKUs that field isn't really meaningful, so it's just noise in the email layout.How to change it:Go to&nbsp;Admin Console → Tools → Company Settings → Email Templates.Under&nbsp;Product Email Item Details, you'll see a line with&nbsp;%upcAndPackQuantity% . Remove that line (or replace it with fields you do want, like description, content, or origin).Under&nbsp;Order Item Line, there's a line that reads&nbsp;UPC: %upcValue%, Pack: %unitsPerCarton% . Remove or edit that the same way if you want order emails cleaned up too.Save, then send a test product email from the iPad to confirm the layout looks right.You can mix and match any of the template variables listed on that page. If you tell us what you'd like to show instead of Pack (e.g., content, origin, minimum yardage), we can suggest the exact lines to paste in.
```

## ✉️ TICKET #15031 [tier A] — 2026-08-12T18:31:54+00:00 — Re: pack

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* dorellfabrics.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* l1 - handled by frontline support, product - admin console, s4 - low, type: training

```
Hi Suzanne,You're correct, that field is empty. I should have been clearer:&nbsp;empty doesn't mean nothing gets printed.&nbsp;When Product Email Item Details is blank, eCat uses the system default layout, and that default includes a Pack line. That's why you're still seeing "Pack: 1" even though you haven't typed anything in.To remove it, paste a custom template into that field (without the Pack variable). Here's a starting point based on your default layout, with Pack removed:&lt;tr&gt;
&lt;td width="300" align="center"&gt;%imageHTML%&lt;/td&gt;
&lt;td width="400"&gt;
&lt;font size=-1&gt;
&lt;p&gt;#%itemNumber%
&lt;br/&gt;%itemDescription%
&lt;br/&gt;%product_dimensions_in%
%volumeLine%
&lt;br/&gt;%materials_description%
&lt;br/&gt;%features%
&lt;br/&gt;%price%
&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;/font&gt;
&lt;/td&gt;
&lt;/tr&gt;
Save, then send a test product email from the iPad to confirm.The&nbsp;Order Item Line&nbsp;note was separate. That field is for order confirmation emails (not product photo emails), and on your account it does have content with&nbsp;UPC: %upcValue%, Pack: %unitsPerCarton% . If you want Pack removed from order emails too, delete that line from Order Item Line and save.
```
